- **prompt-log**: Log an ad-hoc prompt as a polished, self-contained, documentation-quality version — rephrased from the original, with clarifications folded in — together with the agent's findings, what was done, and the iteration it belongs to. Optimized for re-use AND documentation: the saved prompt is what the user *wishes* they had asked. Use when: a coding agent has just finished an ad-hoc task (bug fix, tweak, chore, one-off request), the user wants to capture the prompt that drove it, the user says 'log this prompt' / 'save this' / 'remember this prompt' / 'log this fix'. ([psrank/EditTrail](https://github.com/psrank/EditTrail/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prompt-log))
- **prompt-log-review**: Use when the user runs `/prompt-log-review [세션]`, says "이 세션 채점해줘 / 내 프롬프트 로그 리뷰 / 세션 리뷰해줘", or wants to know which prompting axis they are weakest on. Extracts raw user prompts from a chosen session transcript via a deterministic script (zero token cost), rates them on rate-review's 4 axes in one batch pass, reports the weakest axis plus worst 2-3 prompts with minimal fixes, and appends one ledger line. Never executes the prompts it rates. ([inpilot-dev/prompt-forge](https://github.com/inpilot-dev/prompt-forge/tree/HEAD/skills/prompt-log-review))
- **prompt-logan**: Transforma um pedido cru num prompt pronto para colar num agente de código — direto, sem inventar, só "o que fazer", com critérios de aceite numerados (CA-x) que cobrem 100% do pedido. Antes de escrever, navega na aplicação que o usuário deixou aberta no navegador e, quando o pedido mexe em tela, monta uma maquete da mudança na aba (só visual) e pede aprovação. Guarda o pedido original, a maquete e o prompt final numa pasta de provas. Use quando o usuário invocar /prompt-logan &lt;pedido&gt;.
